### [1] [PROVE: Proof-guided Regime-aware Operator Verification for Hallucination Detection in Medical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29604)
*Keyang Zhou,Siyi Li,Zhongnan Shi,Qichao Ying,Wei Tang,Zhenxing Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PROVE是一个黑盒幻觉检测器，通过将医学VQA问题分为三种验证机制（regime），并针对每个问题自适应地激活和加权五个互补算子，实现对VLM输出的校准风险评估。在三个医学VQA基准和四个前沿VLM上，PROVE达到0.821 AUROC，比最强基线高出0.159。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法对所有问题采用统一的可靠性估计策略（基于响应一致性或视觉证据），忽略了问题特有的特征，导致两类问题：一是对过度自信的错误检测不足（missed overconfident errors），二是因过度验证产生误报（false alarms from over-verification）。

Method: PROVE的核心框架包括：(1) 将问题分类为三种验证机制，依据问题所需的视觉证据类型；(2) 为每种机制激活五个互补算子中的特定子集；(3) 通过轻量级校准层，基于确定性的问题-答案特征调节每个算子的重要性权重；(4) 使用问题特定的证据对算子重新加权，生成校准的风险分数。

Result: 在8048个测试样本上评估，覆盖三个医学VQA基准和四个前沿VLM，PROVE达到0.821 AUROC，超越最强基线0.159，且在所有模型和基准上均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过感知问题特定证据结构的自适应验证策略，PROVE显著提升了医学VQA中的幻觉检测能力，验证了"分而治之"（regime-aware）的验证范式在黑盒场景下的有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: In medical visual question answering (VQA), hallucinations of vision-language models (VLMs) may lead to confident but incorrect responses, raising the risk of diagnostic errors. Existing hallucination detection methods uniformly estimate the reliability of VLM outputs from response consistency or visual evidence. However, such uniform verification across questions ignores question-specific characteristics, resulting in missed overconfident errors and false alarms from over-verification. We present PROVE (Proof-guided Regime-aware Operator Verification), a black-box detector that adapts verification strategy to the evidential structure of each question. PROVE classifies questions into three verification regimes based on what kind of visual proof they demand, activates a regime-specific subset of five complementary operators, and adjusts operator importance per question through a lightweight calibration layer conditioned on deterministic question-answer features. PROVE uses question-specific evidence to reweight operators and produce a calibrated risk score. Evaluated on 8048 test samples across three medical VQA benchmarks and four frontier VLMs, PROVE achieves 0.821 AUROC, outperforming the strongest baseline by +0.159, with consistent gains across all models and benchmarks.

</details>


### [2] [$\unicode{x1F493}$Heartian: Physiology-Aware Relightable Gaussian Head Avatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28539)
*Xiaoyue Fan,Jose Echevarria,Akshay Paruchuri,Kaan Akşit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Heartian，一种生理感知调制框架，用于在可重光照的高斯头部头像中学习与心动周期相关的逐帧面部皮肤区域反照率调制，从而编码可恢复的远程光电容积描记（rPPG）信号。通过同步接触式PPG监督，Heartian将规定的心动波形建模为两个高斯函数之和，并用轻量MLP学习逐帧空间残差。在UBFC-rPPG、PURE和MMPD的152段静态记录上，属性空间恢复心率MAE为0.29 bpm、MAPE为0.38%；渲染后信号仍可被基准rPPG方法检测（最优配置在MMPD上MAE为0.97 bpm），同时PSNR仅下降0.005 dB，维持了与基线相当的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯头部头像通常将内在面部外观建模为时间静态的，忽略了由心脏活动引起的细微皮肤颜色变化。作者旨在将可恢复的rPPG生理信号嵌入到头部头像中，作为可控的材质属性，从而在保持重建质量的同时赋予头像生理感知能力。

Method: Heartian采用生理感知调制框架：学习心动周期依赖的逐帧面部皮肤区域高斯反照率调制；使用同步接触式PPG监督；将规定的心动波形建模为两个高斯函数之和；通过轻量MLP学习逐帧空间残差；将该调制嵌入到可重光照的头部头像中。评估时使用运动增强的TS-CAN解码器等技术进行渲染后信号恢复。

Result: 在152段静态记录（UBFC-rPPG、PURE、MMPD）上，属性空间信号恢复的池化记录级心率MAE为0.29 bpm、MAPE为0.38%；渲染后信号仍可被基准rPPG方法检测，最佳配置（在UBFC-rPPG上预训练的运动增强TS-CAN解码器）从MMPD渲染头像恢复心率达0.97 bpm MAE和1.21% MAPE；同时仅造成0.005 dB的平均PSNR降级。

Conclusion: Heartian成功将可恢复的rPPG信号以可控材质属性的形式嵌入到特定主体的高斯头部头像中，同时保持重建质量几乎不变，实现了生理信号编码与高质量头像重建的兼顾。

Abstract: Gaussian head avatars typically model intrinsic facial appearance as temporally static, omitting subtle cardiac-induced skin-color variation. We propose $\unicode{x1F493}$Heartian, a physiology-aware modulation framework that learns cardiac-cycle-dependent per-frame albedo modulation of facial skin-region Gaussians within a relightable head avatar to encode remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) signals. Using synchronized contact PPG supervision, $\unicode{x1F493}$Heartian models the prescribed cardiac waveform as the sum of two Gaussian functions and learns per-frame spatial residuals via a lightweight MLP. Across 152 stationary recordings from UBFC-rPPG, PURE, and MMPD, attribute-space recovery of the supplied signal achieves a pooled recording-level heart-rate MAE of 0.29 bpm and MAPE of 0.38%. The signals remain detectable after rendering by benchmark rPPG methods, with the best tested configuration - a motion-augmented TS-CAN decoder pretrained on UBFC-rPPG - recovering heart rate from the rendered MMPD avatars at 0.97 bpm MAE and 1.21% MAPE. Meanwhile, $\unicode{x1F493}$Heartian maintains reconstruction quality comparable to the baseline, with negligible average PSNR degradation of 0.005 dB. Overall, our work embeds recoverable rPPG signals as controllable material attributes to subject-specific Gaussian head avatars while retaining the reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [3] [Token Clustering and Semantic Sequence Mamba for Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28580)
*Yimin Zhu,Mahmood Elahi,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STMamba（Token聚类与语义序列Mamba），用于高光谱图像分类。该方法通过密度感知聚类与四叉树动态选择将稀疏token组织成语义连贯的序列，并采用并行时空语义序列建模（SWSM）模块捕获长程依赖，在三个大规模基准数据集上超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像（HSI）虽提供丰富的谱空信息，但受谱空异构性和复杂空间结构影响，像素级分类仍具挑战性。现有视觉状态空间模型（Mamba）通常按预定义空间邻域构建序列，未显式考虑语义相似性及空间非平稳性，限制了建模能力。

Method: STMamba从宏观与微观两个层面设计：(1) 宏观层面，采用层级式编码器-解码器，通过Token聚类模块（TCM）逐步选择语义token，并使用无参数的跨尺度邻域注意力（CNA）上采样器恢复稠密特征；(2) 微观层面，TCM先通过密度感知聚类识别代表性簇中心，再基于特征相似性估计软隶属度，随后利用基于四叉树的动态选择策略从每个语义簇中保留稀疏且空间分布的token，形成连贯的语义token序列并减少像素级冗余表示；(3) 并行空间与光谱语义序列建模Mamba（SWSM）模块在同质语义token序列内捕获互补的长程空间与光谱依赖，同时抑制异质区域间的无关交互。

Result: 在三个大规模基准数据集上的实验结果表明，STMamba在定量与定性指标上均优于现有最优（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: 通过将稀疏token组织成语义连贯序列并抑制异质区域间交互，STMamba有效解决了HSI分类中谱空异构性与空间非平稳性问题，验证了语义序列建模在Mamba框架中的有效性。

Abstract: Although hyperspectral images (HSIs) provide rich spectral-spatial information, accurate pixel-level classification remains challenging because of spectral-spatial heterogeneity and complex spatial structures. Existing vision state space models (Mamba) typically construct sequences according to predefined spatial neighborhoods, without explicitly accounting for semantic similarity or spatial non-stationarity. To address this limitation, we propose Token Clustering and Semantic Sequence Mamba (STMamba), which organizes sparse tokens into semantically coherent sequences for hyperspectral image classification with the following features. First, at the macro level, a hierarchical encoder decoder progressively selects semantic tokens with the Token Clustering Module (TCM) and restores dense features using a parameter-free Cross-scale Neighborhood Attention (CNA) Upsampler. Second, at the micro level, TCM first identifies representative cluster centers through density-aware clustering and estimates soft memberships based on feature similarity. A quadtree-based dynamic selection strategy then retains sparse and spatially distributed tokens from each semantic cluster, forming coherent semantic-token sequences while reducing redundant pixel-wise representations. Third, parallel Spatial and Spectral Semantic-wise Sequencing Mamba (SWSM) modules capture complementary long-range spatial and spectral dependencies within homogeneous semantic token sequences while suppressing irrelevant interactions across heterogeneous regions. Experimental results on three large-scale benchmark datasets demonstrate that STMamba outperforms the SOTA methods with respect to quantitative and qualitative results.

</details>


### [4] [UltraBench 2: Towards Robust Evaluation of Vision Foundation Models on Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28610)
*Ashwath Radhachandran,Adam Tupper,Christian Gagné,William Speier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraBench 2是一个覆盖广泛解剖结构和任务类型的超声影像基础模型综合评测基准，旨在解决当前超声基础模型评估碎片化、不一致的问题。评测结果显示，超声专用预训练模型在分类任务上仍具优势，但通用基础模型在分割任务上已与专用模型持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管近年来超声基础模型不断涌现，但缺乏设计良好的评测基准，导致对竞争模型的评估碎片化且不一致，难以衡量进展。

Method: 构建UltraBench 2基准，具备广泛的解剖覆盖和任务覆盖，专注于标准化、可复现性和易用性，并基于该基准对现有视觉基础模型在超声图像分析任务上进行系统比较。

Result: 超声专用预训练模型在分类任务上仍保持领先；在分割任务上，最先进的通用模型已与超声专用模型达到同等水平。

Conclusion: 超声专用预训练在分类任务仍有价值，但通用基础模型在分割任务上的能力已追平专用模型，这为未来超声基础模型的设计方向提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Benchmarking is an increasingly critical part of research in machine learning and the domains where it is applied, including healthcare. Yet, despite the steady development of new ultrasound foundation models in recent years, the development of well-designed benchmarks to evaluate them has lagged behind. This deficiency has led to fragmented and inconsistent evaluations of competing models, making it difficult to measure progress. To address this issue, we introduce UltraBench 2, a comprehensive benchmark with wide anatomical and task coverage, and a focus on standardization, reproducibility, and ease-of-use. Using this benchmark, we compare existing vision foundation models for ultrasound image analysis. Our analyses demonstrate that ultrasound-specific pretraining still leads on classification, but that state-of-the-art general-purpose models have drawn level on segmentation.

</details>


### [5] [PePESeg3D: Perception Prior Enhances Multi-Scale Segmentation for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28645)
*Sungjae Choi,Seunghee Koh,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PePESeg3D是一个将感知先验注入多尺度3D高斯分割管道的框架，通过将单目深度和掩码约束集成到几何重建中，同时利用密集深度-颜色线索和视图一致的质心监督进行对比特征学习，在SPIn-NeRF、LERF-Mask和NVOS基准上取得了最先进的多尺度分割和场景重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS多尺度分割方法分别重建场景和学习分割特征，导致几何重建不感知语义结构，且特征学习依赖于不完整的掩码监督。作者旨在解决这两个耦合的局限性，通过注入感知先验同时改进几何优化和特征学习。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) PePE Reconstruction，将单目深度和掩码约束集成到上游几何重建中，确保语义一致的物体结构；(2) PePE Contrastive Learning，利用密集深度-颜色线索和视图一致的质心监督进行对比学习，弥补2D基础模型生成的多尺度掩码的不完整性。

Result: 在SPIn-NeRF、LERF-Mask和NVOS三个基准上进行了大量实验，PePESeg3D在多尺度分割和场景重建任务中均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 将感知先验同时集成到几何优化和特征学习中对准确的多尺度3D分割至关重要，验证了语义感知的几何重建与丰富的对比监督相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advancements in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have extended its capabilities to multi-scale segmentation. Existing methods reconstruct a scene with Gaussian primitives and learn multi-scale segmentation features separately, which leaves the geometry unaware of semantic structure and the feature learning dependent on incomplete mask supervision. To address these limitations, we present PePESeg3D, a novel framework that injects perception priors into a multi-scale 3D Gaussian segmentation pipeline. To fully exploit perception priors, we integrate them not only into contrastive feature learning but also into the upstream geometry reconstruction. Specifically, PePE Reconstruction incorporates monocular depth and mask constraints to ensure semantically coherent object structures. Building on this aligned geometry, PePE Contrastive Learning leverages dense depth-color cues and view-consistent centroid supervision to compensate for the incompleteness of multi-scale masks obtained from a 2D foundation model. Extensive experiments on the SPIn-NeRF, LERF-Mask, and NVOS benchmarks demonstrate that PePESeg3D achieves state-of-the-art performance in both multi-scale segmentation and scene reconstruction, highlighting the importance of integrating perception priors into both geometry optimization and feature learning for accurate multi-scale 3D segmentation. Our code is available at https://github.com/BeCow5X5/PePESeg3D.

</details>


### [6] [M-plicits: Neural Implicit Surfaces via Nested Multiscale Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28684)
*Vinícius da Silva,Isabelle Melo,Matheus Bessa,Guilherme Schardong,Luiz Schirmer,André Araújo,Nuno Gonçalves,Hélio Lopes,Alberto Raposo,Luiz Velho,Tiago Novello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出M-plicits，一种多尺度隐式神经表示框架，将表面建模为MLP残差之和，并通过嵌套邻域序列训练，严格将监督限定在前一零级集的窄带内，实现了对噪声的鲁棒性、实时渲染和高精度重建，在Stanford和Thingi32基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式神经表示方法在训练效率、渲染速度和噪声鲁棒性之间难以平衡：单MLP方法推理开销大，基于网格的方法速度虽快但可能限制表面光滑性并过拟合噪声，而此前多尺度方法因硬频谱截断容易捕获噪声并产生伪影。

Method: 提出M-plicits多尺度框架，将表面建模为多个MLP的残差之和，通过嵌套邻域序列训练。与现有残差方法不同，该方法将监督严格限定在前一零级集的窄带内；粗网络充当低通滤波器提供干净的几何先验，后续残差逐步细化几何而不过拟合高频伪影。此外引入多尺度球追踪算法和基于GEMM的解析法线计算，完全绕过自动微分。

Result: 在Stanford和Thingi32基准上，M-plicits在粗配置下取得最佳平均Chamfer距离，在细配置下取得最佳中位Chamfer距离和IoU；相比iNGP、BACON和IDF具有显著更好的噪声鲁棒性，且参数量比基于网格的基线少一个数量级。

Conclusion: M-plicits通过窄带局部监督的多尺度残差设计，同时实现了高精度重建、实时渲染和强噪声鲁棒性，为隐式神经表示提供了一种平衡且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Encoding input coordinates with sinusoidal functions into multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) has proven effective for implicit neural representations (INRs) of surfaces defined as zero-level sets. However, existing methods often struggle to balance training efficiency, rendering speed, and noise robustness: single-MLP approaches are expensive at inference, grid-based representations are fast but can limit surface smoothness and overfit input noise, and previous multiscale approaches frequently capture noise and produce artifacts due to hard spectral truncation. To address these limitations, we propose M-plicits, a multiscale framework that models surfaces as a residual sum of MLPs trained via a sequence of nested neighborhoods. Unlike existing residual approaches that rely on standard domain-wide sampling and require costly mesh extraction for visualization, our method strictly localizes supervision to narrow bands around the previous zero-level sets. This nested design naturally provides robustness against noisy input data: the coarse network acts as a low-pass filter that establishes a clean geometric prior, while subsequent residuals progressively refine the geometry without fitting to high-frequency artifacts. We further introduce a multiscale sphere-tracing algorithm and a GEMM-based analytical normal computation that bypasses auto-differentiation entirely, yielding high-fidelity real-time rendering. On Stanford and Thingi32, M-plicits achieves the best mean Chamfer distance in the coarse configuration and the best median Chamfer distance and IoU in the fine configuration, with substantially better noise robustness than iNGP, BACON, and IDF, while using an order of magnitude fewer parameters than grid-based baselines. Code, models, and data will be released at https://github.com/dsilvavinicius/m-plicits.

</details>


### [7] [GeoNLI - A Natural Language Interpreter for Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28741)
*Ashutosh Gandhe,Anupam Rawat,Geet Sethi,Kabir Nasiruddin,Madhav Kotecha,Panav Shah,Rakshit Sawarn,Soumitra Nayak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的模块化流水线，将先进的SAM变体与多模态大语言模型结合，在遥感数据集上联合执行图像描述、视觉问答和视觉定位任务。通过多数投票集成多个模型（EarthMind、RemoteSAM、SAM3、Falcon、DiffuSAM等），系统在描述任务上达到82%准确率，VQA达到83.32%，定位达到64.94%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态多任务模型在遥感数据集上表现强劲，但由于训练数据异质性和任务差异，设计一个在图像描述、视觉问答（VQA）和视觉定位上均表现良好的统一模型仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 评估多个模型在VRS Bench和NWPU-VHR-10数据集上的表现。EarthMind在描述和VQA上表现优异；针对定位任务，提出RemoteSAM-SAM-v1、RemoteSAM-SAM-v2和DiffuSAM等多个流水线，最终采用跨EarthMind、RemoteSAM、SAM3、Falcon、RemoteSAM-SAM3-v1、RemoteSAM-SAM3-v2和DiffuSAM预测的多数投票集成方案。

Result: 统一模块化流水线在描述任务上达到82%准确率，VQA达83.32%（其中二元问题90.94%、数值问题52.04%、语义问题92.06%），视觉定位达64.94%准确率。

Conclusion: 通过集成多数投票结合多样化VLM与自定义RemoteSAM-SAM3模型，该系统比单一任务专用方法在遥感理解上提供更准确、更一致的结果。

Abstract: Multi-modal multitasking models have shown strong performance on remote sensing datasets. However, because these models are trained on heterogeneous data and vary across tasks, designing a unified model that performs well in captioning, visual question answering (VQA), and visual grounding remains challenging.
  In this work, we evaluate several models on the VRS Bench and NWPU-VHR-10 datasets. The EarthMind model demonstrates strong results in both captioning and VQA. For grounding, we propose multiple pipelines - RemoteSAM-SAM-v1, RemoteSAM-SAM-v2, and DiffuSAM - and ultimately adopt a majority-voting ensemble across EarthMind, RemoteSAM, SAM3, Falcon, RemoteSAM-SAM3-v1, RemoteSAM-SAM3-v2, and DiffuSAM predictions.
  Our unified, modular pipeline integrates advanced SAM variants with multimodal LLMs to jointly perform captioning, VQA, and grounding. It achieves 82% accuracy on captioning and 83.32% on VQA, with 90.94%, 52.04%, and 92.06% for binary, numeric, and semantic question types respectively. For grounding, it attains 64.94% accuracy. By combining diverse VLMs with our custom RemoteSAM-SAM3 models through ensemble majority voting, the system delivers more accurate and consistent remote-sensing understanding than task-specific approaches.

</details>


### [8] [Small yet Assistive: Spatially-Aware Post-Training for Low Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28757)
*Rishabh Choudhary,Shreyansh Raj,Umesh Goyal,Shubh Kashyap,Shrestha Kumar,Sushovan Jena,Komal Kumar,Hisham Cholakkal,Aditya Nigam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Smol-VL-BLV，一个专为盲人和低视力（BLV）用户设计的紧凑型视觉语言模型（约500M参数），通过教师-学生蒸馏和GRPO强化学习等两阶段后训练机制，在移动设备上实现具备空间细节、方向提示和危险感知能力的图像描述生成，同时保持低延迟和离线运行能力，在空间评分、社交评分、OCR和TextVQA等基准上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球约10亿人患有视力障碍，但现有的视觉语言模型（VLMs）生成的描述过于模糊，无法为BLV用户提供安全的导航辅助。大型VLM能生成高质量的音频描述合规叙述但无法在移动设备上运行；小型VLM延迟虽低但缺乏空间细节、方向线索和危险感知能力。因此需要一个能在移动设备上实时运行的紧凑型VLM来弥合这一差距。

Method: 使用500M解码器Transformer模型，采用两种后训练机制：(1) 教师-学生蒸馏；(2) 基于复合BLV奖励的GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization），该奖励针对方向语言、度量距离和危险检测。为缓解多阶段后训练导致的灾难性遗忘，在最终GRPO微调后添加了轻量级微调阶段以恢复通用描述质量，同时保持BLV特定的空间接地能力。部署上采用混合精度量化，模型约450MB。

Result: 模型在多种基准上显著优于基线：空间评分相对提升19.3%，社交评分提升14.8%，OCR-Bench提升101.5%，TextVQA准确率提升44.2%。模型部署于中端Android智能手机，约450MB，完全离线运行，延迟取决于主机硬件能力。

Conclusion: BLV聚焦的后训练不仅能提升无障碍相关的空间接地能力，还能改善通用的视觉文本推理能力。该工作表明紧凑型VLM通过针对性的后训练可以在移动设备上实现安全导航所需的描述质量，模型、数据集和代码已公开发布。

Abstract: An estimated 1 billion people worldwide live with vision impairment, yet current vision-language models (VLMs) produce descriptions too vague for safe navigation by blind and low-vision (BLV) users. Large VLMs can generate high-quality audio-description-compliant narrations but cannot run on mobile devices; small VLMs offer competitive latency but lack spatial detail, directional cues, and hazard awareness for navigational assistance. We present Smol-VL-BLV, a compact VLM for blind and low-vision users that closes this gap using a 500M decoder transformer model and two post-training mechanisms: (1) teacher-student distillation and (2) Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a composite BLV reward targeting directional language, metric distances, and hazard detection. Because multi-stage post-training can induce catastrophic forgetting, we add a lightweight finetuning stage after the last stage GRPO finetuning to recover general descriptive quality while preserving BLV-specific spatial grounding. Our best model substantially outperforms the baseline across various benchmarks, including tasks: VQA, BLV captioning, OCR, and latency. Compared with the baseline for relative improvement, it improves the Spatial score gain of 19.3%, and the Social score gain of 14.8%. It also increases OCR-Bench by 101.5%, and raises TextVQA accuracy by 44.2%. These results show that BLV-focused post-training improves both accessibility-specific spatial grounding and general visual-text reasoning. Deployed on a mid-range Android smartphone via Mixed-Precision Quantization, the model remains approx. 450 MB and runs entirely on-device, offline and without network dependency, generating descriptions with latency dependent on host hardware capabilities. Our model, dataset, and code is publicly released at https://smol-vl-blv.github.io/Smol-VL-BLV-website/

</details>


### [9] [DrGait: Biomechanically Grounded Visual Reasoning for Interpretable Clinical Gait Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28796)
*Xiangyu Yin,Shiqi Wang,Abrar Alamri,Yasir Aljohani,Weichen Liu,Goeran Fiedler,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DrGait是一个无需训练的智能体框架，通过将VLM从直接视觉推理者转变为临床规划者，采用分流-验证-综合(TVS)工作流解耦语义推理与几何感知，利用确定性生物力学工具验证诊断假设，从而减少幻觉并生成透明可审计的步态分析临床报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化步态分析依赖不可解释的黑盒分类器，而VLM虽具备强推理能力，但直接应用于步态视频时因难以从原始视觉上下文中测量细微几何偏差而产生幻觉，亟需一种既能利用VLM推理能力又能保证几何测量可靠性的方法。

Method: DrGait采用训练无关的智能体框架，核心是Triage-Verification-Synthesis (TVS)工作流：(1)启发式分流提出诊断假设；(2)自主调用确定性生物力学工具，对重建的3D网格轨迹、分割的2D姿态轨迹和以事件为中心的视频证据进行验证；(3)闭环机制根据反馈递归更新推理上下文，将VLM推理锚定在可验证的几何和时间测量上。

Result: DrGait在保持竞争性诊断准确率的同时，显著减少了幻觉，并生成透明且可审计的临床报告。

Conclusion: 通过将VLM的角色从直接视觉推理者转变为临床规划者，并将语义推理与几何感知解耦，DrGait为临床步态分析提供了一种可解释、可靠且无需训练的解决方案，兼顾推理能力与可验证性。

Abstract: Current automated gait analysis for clinical applications relies on uninterpretable black-box classifiers. Although Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer strong reasoning capabilities, applying them directly to gait videos often leads to hallucinations, because they struggle to measure subtle geometric deviations from raw visual contexts. To address this, we introduce DrGait, a training-free agentic framework that shifts the VLM's role from a direct visual reasoner to a clinical planner. DrGait decouples semantic reasoning from geometric perception through a structured Triage-Verification-Synthesis (TVS) workflow. Given an input video and a set of basic spatiotemporal metrics, the DrGait agent first performs a heuristic triage to propose diagnostic hypotheses, which are then verified by autonomously calling deterministic biomechanical tools that operate on reconstructed 3D mesh trajectories, segmented 2D pose tracks, and event-centered video evidence. Finally, a closed-loop mechanism recursively updates the agent's reasoning context based on the feedback. By anchoring VLM's reasoning in verifiable geometric and temporal measurements, DrGait reduces hallucinations, achieving competitive diagnostic accuracy while generating transparent and audit-ready clinical reports.

</details>


### [10] [DeltaWAM: Delta World Action Models for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28811)
*Han Yan,Zishang Xiang,Haokai Jiang,Zeyu Zhang,Qilin Wang,Weiyu Guo,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeltaWAM是一种新的世界动作模型，通过联合预测视觉增量（visual deltas）和动作，避免密集未来帧预测带来的高计算开销。它采用密集锚点、稀疏增量和动作三个流，并提出流式增量记忆（SDM）机制来更新缓存的锚点上下文，显著降低推理延迟和计算量。在RoboTwin基准和真实机器人评估中，DeltaWAM+SDM在成功率、训练FLOPs和推理效率上均优于现有方法Fast-WAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）在训练时需要预测密集的未来帧，反复建模大量不变的内容，将动作条件动力学与无关的外观变化耦合在一起；在推理时，处理每个完整观察需要依赖重型视频专家，成为少步动作生成的瓶颈。

Method: 提出DeltaWAM，联合预测视觉增量和动作，采用三个数据流：密集锚点（dense-anchor）、稀疏增量（sparse-delta）和动作（action）流，并设计了三种在表征与计算共享方面不同的架构。进一步提出流式增量记忆（SDM），用紧凑的观测增量更新缓存的锚点上下文，减少重型视频专家的处理量。

Result: 在RoboTwin基准上，DeltaWAM+SDM相比Fast-WAM将平均成功率从81.3%提升到85.4%（干净环境），从75.8%提升到83.9%（视觉随机化环境）。三种架构降低训练FLOPs 17.78%-23.77%，SDM分别降低一步推理延迟和FLOPs 36.57%和31.55%。真实机器人评估取得最高整体成功率和归一化进度。

Conclusion: 通过预测视觉增量而非完整密集帧，并配合流式增量记忆机制，DeltaWAM在保持世界动作模型优势的同时显著提升了训练和推理效率，并在仿真与真实机器人任务中均取得更好性能。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) transfer visual and motion priors from pretrained video generators to robot control by jointly modeling visual dynamics and actions. Existing WAMs, however, predict dense future frames during training, repeatedly modeling largely unchanged content and coupling action-conditioned dynamics to nuisance appearance variations. At inference, processing each complete observation with the heavy video expert bottlenecks few-step action generation. Accordingly, we propose DeltaWAM, which jointly predicts visual deltas and actions using dense-anchor, sparse-delta, and action streams, with three architectures that differ in representation and computation sharing. We further develop Streaming Delta Memory (SDM), which updates cached anchor context with compact observed deltas, reducing heavy video-expert processing. On RoboTwin, DeltaWAM with SDM improves average success over Fast-WAM from 81.3% to 85.4% in the clean setting and from 75.8% to 83.9% under visual randomization. The three architectures reduce training FLOPs by 17.78-23.77%, while SDM reduces one-step inference latency and FLOPs by 36.57% and 31.55%, respectively; real-world evaluations further show the highest overall success rate and normalized progress among the evaluated policies. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/DeltaWAM. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/DeltaWAM.

</details>


### [11] [CinematicVQA: Benchmarking Film-Grammar Reasoning in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28813)
*Shuo Xing,Pooja Verlani,Balu Adsumilli,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CinematicVQA，首个面向电影语法推理的视频理解基准，引入电影场景图（CSG）将拍摄技术与感知效果、叙事功能联系起来。评估发现LVLMs在描述视觉呈现上表现较好但在识别底层技术上存在显著语义差距，CoT提示无法带来一致收益，而微调能持续提升模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频问答基准主要关注低级技术识别（如镜头类型、灯光），缺乏对电影叙事影响和电影语法推理的评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个能够评估LVLMs对电影语言深层理解能力的基准。

Method: （1）提出CinematicVQA基准，首个评估电影语法推理的视频理解基准；（2）引入电影场景图（CSG），一种结构化表示，将拍摄技术与感知效果及叙事功能相关联；（3）对多种SOTA LVLMs进行全面评估，包括CoT提示和微调实验。

Result: 模型在描述视觉呈现上的表现始终优于识别底层技术，揭示了显著的语义差距；CoT提示未能带来一致增益且对多数模型反而降低性能，说明当前LVLMs缺乏足够的电影领域知识来从逐步推理中获益；在CinematicVQA-train上微调带来一致改进，尤其在叙事功能和多跳推理任务上。

Conclusion: CinematicVQA既可作为LVLMs电影评估的严格基准，也可作为训练更具电影感知能力的视频模型的实际数据集，推动LVLMs向更深层次的电影语言理解发展。

Abstract: Cinematography, the craft of visual storytelling through framing, lighting, and camera operation, fundamentally shapes how audiences perceive and emotionally engage with video content. While Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) have made remarkable progress in video question answering, existing benchmarks primarily focus on identifying low-level techniques rather than understanding their storytelling impact. To address this, we introduce CinematicVQA, the first-of-its-kind benchmark for cinematic video understanding that goes beyond technique recognition to evaluate film-grammar reasoning, utilizing our introduced Cinematic Scene Graph (CSG), a structured representation that links filming techniques to their perceptual effects and narrative functions. Through comprehensive evaluation of state-of-the-art LVLMs, we reveal a striking semantic gap: models consistently perform higher on describing visual presentations than on identifying the underlying techniques. Surprisingly, Chain-of-Thought prompting fails to provide consistent gains and degrades performance for most models, suggesting that current LVLMs lack sufficient cinematic domain knowledge to benefit from step-by-step reasoning. Fine-tuning on \textsc{CinematicVQA-train} yields consistent improvements, particularly for narrative function and multi-hop reasoning. Overall, \textsc{CinematicVQA} serves both as a rigorous benchmark for cinematic evaluation in LVLMs and as a practical dataset for training more film-aware video models.

</details>


### [12] [M$^2$PFN: End-to-End Disentangled Alignment for Generalizable Multimodal In-Context Learning in Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28836)
*Lujia Zhong,Shuo Huang,Jianwei Zhang,Xinyu Nie,Yonggang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出M²PFN框架，将表格式基础模型TabPFN的上下文学习（ICL）能力扩展到多模态阿尔茨海默病（AD）诊断中。通过端到端训练将3D-MRI和表格特征编码器与冻结的TabPFN引擎对齐，实现跨队列泛化的多模态AD分类和MMSE回归，在ADNI、OASIS-3和SCAN三个队列上均超越多种单模态和多模态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结合影像和表格数据的多模态AD诊断方法在跨队列泛化上表现有限。TabPFN等表格式基础模型展现出优异的上下文学习能力和灵活性，但其元训练基于合成表格先验，与图像衍生特征的统计结构不匹配，难以直接应用于多模态AD分析。

Method: M²PFN包含三个关键组件：(i) 通过TabPFN的Transformer进行可微推理，将任务梯度反向传播到3D-MRI和表格编码器；(ii) 通过解缠和对比学习目标将两种模态对齐到与ICL引擎先验匹配的共享子空间；(iii) 通过可学习的门控捷径融合冻结的纯表格预测。ICL引擎保持冻结以保留测试时泛化能力，而端到端训练使编码器生成可利用的特征。

Result: 在ADNI（n=2240，三分类CN/MCI/AD）上，M²PFN达到65.55%的macro-F1和82.21%的macro-AUC，超越所有单模态和多模态基线。将分类头替换为TabPFN回归器后，在1250名受试者的子队列上回归基线MMSE，测试MAE为1.743，优于所有多模态基线。在两个外部队列（OASIS-3和SCAN）上无重训练即取得最佳AUC和最低MMSE MAE，且即使认知工具发生变化也能迁移。

Conclusion: M²PFN成功将表格式基础模型的上下文学习能力扩展到多模态医学影像分析中，通过保持ICL引擎冻结并端到端训练编码器，实现了优异的跨队列泛化性能，为多模态AD诊断提供了新的范式。

Abstract: While various multimodal methods combining imaging and tabular data for Alzheimer's disease (AD) diagnosis were proposed, they are often limited in generalization across cohorts. In-context learning (ICL) has demonstrated excellent generalization performances and high flexibility in foundational tabular models such as TabPFN. To extend TabPFN's ICL to multimodal AD analysis, the main obstacle is that TabPFN is meta-trained on synthetic tabular priors that do not naturally match the statistical structure of image-derived features. We propose M$^2$PFN, an end-to-end framework that turns this tabular foundation model into a multimodal AD predictor. M$^2$PFN (i) performs differentiable inference through TabPFN's transformer, back-propagating task gradients into 3D-MRI and tabular encoders; (ii) aligns the two modalities into a shared subspace, via disentanglement and a contrastive objective, matched to the ICL engine's prior; and (iii) folds in a frozen tabular-only prediction through a learnable gated shortcut. Because the ICL engine stays frozen, its in-context mechanism is preserved for test-time generalization, while end-to-end training shapes the encoders into features it can exploit. On ADNI ($n=2240$, three-class CN/MCI/AD), M$^2$PFN attains $65.55\%$ macro-F1 and $82.21\%$ macro-AUC, surpassing a comprehensive set of unimodal and multimodal baselines. By swapping only the head for a TabPFN regressor, the same architecture regresses baseline MMSE on a $1250$-subject sub-cohort to test MAE $1.743$, outperforming every multimodal baseline. On two external cohorts (OASIS-3 and SCAN) with no retraining, M$^2$PFN achieves the best AUC and the lowest MMSE MAE across all baselines, and transfers even when the cognitive instrument changes.

</details>


### [13] [Looks the Same, Answers Differently: Flip-Direction Steering for Robust Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28851)
*Yeonsung Jung,Joonhyun Jeong,Hoang Pham,Joowon Kim,Yoonsik Park,Viet Dac Lai,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlipDir，一种无需训练的推理时方法，通过估计低秩翻转诱导激活子空间并在解码时选择性地抑制隐藏状态，解决视觉语言模型在长程生成中因细微视觉变化导致的"答案翻转"不稳定性问题。同时提出VisFlip基准框架，用于在九种数据集-变化组合上分别评估原始预测恢复和稳定预测保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）在视觉推理上表现强劲，但日常图像采集和处理中的细微变化（即使图像看起来几乎相同）会改变模型的推理轨迹。在长程生成中，这些激活偏移会逐解码步累积，逐步改变推理词元并最终改变最终答案（称为"答案翻转"现象），导致模型在细微视觉扰动下推理不稳定。

Method: 提出FlipDir（Flip-Direction Steering）方法：(1) 从原始输入与答案翻转输入的对比对中估计低秩翻转诱导激活子空间；(2) 在解码过程中选择性地引导隐藏状态，通过边距（margin）门控机制仅在不稳定的解码步骤衰减子空间成分，从而恢复原始预测同时保留稳定步骤。此外提出VisFlip基准框架，为特定目标模型和视觉变化设置构建评估组，分别衡量原始预测的恢复能力和稳定预测的保持能力。

Result: VisFlip涵盖科学推理、机器人场景理解和医学VQA九种数据集-变化组合。在18个实验设置中，FlipDir在组合的恢复（recovery）和保持（preservation）指标上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FlipDir作为一种训练免费的推理时干预方法，有效缓解了VLMs因细微视觉变化引起的推理不稳定问题，同时VisFlip提供了更细粒度的鲁棒性评估框架，超越传统仅看准确率或固定测试集一致性的评估方式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong visual reasoning performance, yet subtle changes from routine image capture and processing can alter their reasoning trajectories even when images appear nearly identical. In long-horizon generation, the resulting activation shifts may accumulate across decoding steps, progressively altering reasoning tokens and ultimately changing the final answer, a phenomenon referred to as answer flips. To address this instability, we propose FlipDir (Flip-Direction Steering), a training-free inference-time method that estimates a low-rank flip-inducing activation subspace from contrastive pairs of original and answer-flipping inputs and selectively steers hidden states during decoding. A margin-based gate limits subspace attenuation to uncertain decoding steps, recovering original predictions while preserving stable ones. To evaluate robustness beyond accuracy or consistency on fixed test sets, we introduce VisFlip, a benchmark framework that constructs evaluation groups for a target model and visual variation setting to separately assess recovery of original predictions and preservation of stable ones. VisFlip spans nine dataset-variation combinations across scientific reasoning, robot-scene understanding, and medical VQA, covering subtle visual variations common in each domain. Experiments across 18 settings demonstrate that FlipDir consistently outperforms existing methods on the combined recovery and preservation metric. We will make our code publicly available.

</details>


### [14] [MEVL-STP: Multi-Encoder and Vision Language Model for Arbitrarily Shaped Scene Text Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28857)
*Aman Anand,Partha Pratim Roy,Shivakumara Palaiahnakote*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段场景文本识别pipeline，将多编码器分割（六个冻结视觉编码器融合）与视觉语言模型识别（Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct + LoRA微调）相结合，用于任意形状文本（如弯曲和密集多方向文本）的检测与识别。在CTW1500上取得91.99%检测F-measure和85.86%端到端H-mean，无需合成预训练数据即达最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景文本识别对任意形状文本（如弯曲标志、密集多方向字符）仍具挑战性，紧密耦合的架构会将定位误差直接传播为识别失败，且现有方法在复杂弯曲文本上的边界精度和识别鲁棒性不足。

Method: 两阶段pipeline：(1) 检测阶段采用六个冻结的视觉编码器（CLIP、DINOv2、SigLIP、EVA-CLIP、SAM、ConvNeXt）提取互补特征，通过可训练的分层FPN（含通道注意力）融合，并由深度监督的渐进式尺度扩展网络（PSENet）解码生成实例级文本掩码；(2) 识别阶段将多边形掩码裁剪的文本区域输入经LoRA微调的Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型进行识别。冻结编码器保持特征空间正交性，避免特征同质化。

Result: 在CTW1500上达到91.99%检测F-measure和85.86%端到端H-mean，创下新SOTA；在Total-Text和ICDAR 2015上也表现强劲；整个方法无需任何合成预训练数据。

Conclusion: 通过多编码器特征融合解决单骨干网络特征同质化问题，并通过多边形掩码裁剪隔离文本与背景干扰，再结合视觉语言模型实现鲁棒的任意形状文本识别，验证了无需合成数据的可行性。

Abstract: Scene text spotting remains challenging for arbitrarily shaped text instances such as curved signs and dense multi-oriented characters in natural images, where tightly coupled architectures propagate localization errors directly into recognition failures. We present a two-stage pipeline that combines multi-encoder segmentation with vision-language model recognition to address this problem. In the detection stage, six frozen vision encoders (CLIP, DINOv2, SigLIP, EVA-CLIP, SAM, and ConvNeXt) extract complementary features spanning semantic, spatial, and texture spectra, which are fused through a trainable hierarchical Feature Pyramid Network with channel attention and decoded via a deep-supervision Progressive Scale Expansion network to generate precise instance-level text masks. By keeping the encoders frozen, their independently learned feature spaces remain orthogonal during fusion, preventing the feature homogenization that degrades boundary precision in single-backbone detectors. The detection stage produces tight polygon masks that conform to the actual shape of curved and arbitrarily oriented text, rather than axis-aligned rectangles that inevitably include background content. In the recognition stage, these polygon-masked crops isolate the target text from surrounding clutter, allowing a Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct model, fine-tuned via Low-Rank Adaptation on polygon-cropped scene text, to focus purely on reading the text without interference from neighbouring words or background noise. Without any synthetic pretraining data, our method achieves 91.99% detection F-measure and 85.86% end-to-end H-mean on CTW1500, setting a new state of the art and achieving strong performance on Total-Text and ICDAR 2015 without any synthetic training data. Code is available at https://github.com/doubleblind-afk/MEVL-STP

</details>


### [15] [Multimodal Routing and Region Refinement for Language-Guided Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28860)
*Md Maklachur Rahman,Md Hasan Al Banna,Saraf Anjum,Assame Arnob,Tracy Hammond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MRSeg是一个参数高效的医学图像分割框架，利用每对图像-文本描述来动态路由视觉和文本特征的适配过程，通过联合路由器在低秩适配器基座上产生稀疏混合，协调跨多个尺度的特征适配，并在QaTa-COV19和MosMedData+数据集上以7.11M可训练参数实现优于现有方法的Dice/mIoU性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本引导医学图像分割方法主要改进图像与语言特征的交互方式，但普遍对所有的图像-文本对保留单一固定的学习更新路径，缺乏针对不同输入对的动态适配能力，限制了分割的准确性与适应性。

Method: MRSeg采用冻结的ConvNeXt-Tiny和PubMedBERT编码器分别提取多尺度视觉特征和临床文本token。联合路由器利用最深视觉特征与池化文本预测低秩适配器基座上的稀疏混合，该路由在两组视觉尺度和文本的适配器组间共享以协调适配，同时保持特征特有参数分离。Region Bridge以文本派生查询聚合密集视觉token形成潜在区域，经自注意力与文本交叉注意力细化后重新分配回特征图。多尺度解码器结合细化语义特征与浅层图像证据完成分割。

Result: 在QaTa-COV19和MosMedData+数据集上，MRSeg分别达到90.90/83.32和81.53/68.82的Dice/mIoU，仅使用7.11M可训练参数和7.60 GFLOPs计算量，实现了参数的显著节省与性能提升。

Conclusion: MRSeg展示了利用图像-文本对动态路由特征适配的有效性，在保持参数高效的同时显著提升了文本引导医学图像分割的性能，为多模态医学图像分割提供了一种新的参数高效范式。

Abstract: Textual descriptions can reduce ambiguity in medical image segmentation by specifying the finding and location to be delineated. Existing text-guided methods mainly improve where image and language features interact but generally retain a single learned update pathway across all image-text pairs. We propose MRSeg, a parameter-efficient framework that uses each image-text pair to route the adaptation of visual and textual features before dense prediction. Frozen ConvNeXt-Tiny and PubMedBERT encoders provide multiscale visual features and clinical text tokens. A joint router uses the deepest visual feature and pooled text to predict a sparse mixture over low-rank adapter bases. The resulting route is shared across separate adapter banks for two visual scales and text, coordinating their adaptation while keeping the feature-specific parameters separate. Region Bridge uses text-derived queries to aggregate dense visual tokens into latent regions, refines these regions through self-attention and text cross-attention, and redistributes the refined information back to the feature maps. Finally, a multiscale decoder combines refined semantic features with shallow image evidence. On QaTa-COV19 and MosMedData+, MRSeg achieves 90.90/83.32 and 81.53/68.82 Dice/mIoU, respectively, with 7.11M trainable parameters and 7.60 GFLOPs. Code: https://github.com/maklachur/MRSeg.

</details>


### [16] [Direction-Scale Decomposition in Action Representation: Rethinking What to Tokenize for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28865)
*Yufei Duan,Hang Yin,Alberta Longhini,Chao Tang,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出方向-尺度分解（DSD）动作表示方法，将平移和旋转增量分解为方向和尺度分量后再进行token化，以隔离运动方向并保留幅值信息，在LIBERO、SimplerEnv和真实机器人实验中均验证了其对离散token VLA模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离散token视觉-语言-动作（VLA）学习中，动作表示起着核心作用但尚未被充分研究。传统的位姿增量表示对执行速度和数据集特定归一化敏感，可能掩盖跨演示和数据集共享的几何结构。

Method: 引入方向-尺度分解（DSD），将平移和旋转增量分解为方向分量和尺度分量后再进行token化，方向被隔离而幅值保留在独立的尺度通道中。使用均匀分箱（BIN）和基于B样条的BEAST tokenizer在仿真和真实环境中评估，涵盖单数据集和混合数据集训练场景。

Result: 在LIBERO上，DSD在两种tokenizer下均提升了平均成功率；在SimplerEnv上，混合数据集训练下DSD-BIN比BIN在总体成功率上提升10.3个百分点；真实机器人实验在有无机器人预训练条件下均取得增益。

Conclusion: DSD是一种有效的离散token VLA模型动作表示方法，并有望缓解在大型多样化混合数据集上训练时的性能退化问题。

Abstract: Action representation plays a central role in discrete-token vision-language-action (VLA) learning but remains underexamined. Under conventional pose-increment representations, action tokens are sensitive to execution speed and dataset-specific normalization, potentially obscuring geometric structure shared across demonstrations and datasets. We introduce Direction-Scale Decomposition (DSD), an action representation that decomposes translation and rotation increments into direction and scale components before tokenization. DSD isolates motion direction while retaining magnitudes in separate scale channels. We evaluate DSD with uniform binning (BIN) and BEAST, a B-spline-based tokenizer, in simulation and real-world manipulation under both single-dataset and mixed-dataset training. On LIBERO, DSD improves average success rates with both tokenizers. On SimplerEnv, DSD-BIN outperforms BIN by 10.3 percentage points in overall success rate under mixed-dataset training. Real-robot experiments further show gains both with and without robotics pretraining. These results support DSD as an effective action representation for discrete-token VLA models and suggest its potential to mitigate performance degradation when training on large and diverse dataset mixtures. Our project page with additional resources is available at https://vla-dsd.github.io/

</details>


### [17] [ViRDM: Taming Representation Distribution Matching for Few-Step Causal Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28923)
*Zichong Meng,Chongjian Ge,Chun-Hao P. Huang,Yang Zhou,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ViRDM，一种免教师、免评判器的视频后训练方案，将原本需要"大预训练教师+在线评判器"三网络的 DMD 蒸馏，简化为仅针对生成器的后训练。通过将图像单步生成中的表征分布匹配（RDM）迁移到少步因果视频生成，逐一解决三大障碍（存储不可行的梯度路径、特有的视频优化机制、表征分布对时间动态约束不足），在 20 次生成器更新、16 A100 GPU 小时内于 VBench 达到 84.87，超越先前最优少步因果基线 0.36。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步自回归视频扩散可实现低延迟流式生成，但现有后训练方法（如 DMD）主要依赖大预训练教师网络与在线评判器，通过扩散分数估计分布差异，资源开销巨大。作者探索能否去除这一教师-评判器堆栈，仅对生成器在预计算目标分布上进行后训练，以降低训练成本并提升可扩展性。

Method: ViRDM 将单步图像生成中的表征分布匹配（RDM）系统性地迁移到少步因果视频生成，并针对三个障碍提出对策：1）将 RDM 与随机截断的 clean-exit 监督相结合，解决存储不可行的梯度路径问题；2）采用轻量 VAE 解码器与分阶段向量-雅可比积（VJP），使多步因果视频展开的 RDM 在内存上可行；3）确立有效的生成群体（generated-population）与初始化机制，并引入轻量动态正则化，补偿表征分布对时间动态约束不足的问题。

Result: ViRDM 将三网络蒸馏转化为仅生成器后训练，显著降低 GPU 内存占用与训练时间，同时提升视频质量。仅 20 次生成器更新即可在官方 VBench 评估上达到 84.87，超过先前最优少步因果基线 0.36，仅需 16 A100 GPU 小时。探索性实验还显示该方案在更低因果采样预算以及一步、两步、四步双向生成上具有潜力。

Conclusion: 作者证明了少步因果视频生成的后训练可以完全摆脱资源密集的教师-评判器架构，仅通过针对预计算目标分布的生成器后训练（表征分布匹配）即可实现高质量、低成本的视频生成，为高效视频扩散后训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive (AR) video diffusion enables low-latency streaming generation, but existing post-training methods predominantly rely on Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD), requiring both a large pretrained teacher and an online critic to estimate distributional discrepancies through diffusion scores. In this work, we ask whether this resource-intensive teacher--critic stack can be eliminated by post-training only the generator against a precomputed target distribution. Drawing inspiration from representation distribution matching (RDM) for one-step image generation, we systematically study its transfer to few-step causal video generation and identify three key barriers: a memory-intractable gradient path, a distinct video optimization regime, and representation distributions that underconstrain temporal dynamics. We introduce ViRDM, a teacher- and critic-free video post-training recipe that addresses these barriers sequentially. By coupling RDM with stochastically truncated clean-exit supervision, a lightweight VAE decoder, and staged vector--Jacobian products, ViRDM makes representation distribution matching memory-feasible for multi-step causal video rollouts. We further establish effective generated-population and initialization regimes for video RDM, and introduce lightweight dynamics regularization to compensate for the underconstrained temporal dynamics. ViRDM turns three-network distillation into generator-only post-training, reducing GPU memory use and training time while improving video quality. With only 20 generator updates, the recipe reaches 84.87 on the official VBench evaluation, outperforming the previous best few-step causal baseline by 0.36, while requiring 16 A100 GPU-hours. We additionally report exploratory results demonstrating the potential of the same recipe for lower causal sampling budget and for one-, two-, and four-step bidirectional generation.

</details>


### [18] [PlenoCI: Plenoptic CharacterIstics for View Dependence Aware Change Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28930)
*Jason Lai,Chamuditha Jayanga Galappaththige,Niko Suenderhauf,Dimity Miller,Donald G. Dansereau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PlenoCI（Plenoptic CharacterIstics），一种基于光场表示的5D结构特征，通过从3D Gaussian Splatting表示中推导闭式解析光场导数来捕获丰富的视觉行为（如遮挡和视角依赖），同时忽略朗伯纹理。该方法对欠约束表示具有鲁棒性，在独立重建的未变化场景中误报率比其他方法低两个数量级。在变化分类任务上，PlenoCI在CL-Splats基准上以25.7% mIoU提升超越最强竞争者达到SOTA，在PASLCD基准上也保持竞争力；在几何/外观变化分类上达到0.735的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting等辐射场表示虽然能原生编码遮挡和视角依赖等复杂视觉现象，但其本质上是欠约束的——即使在未变化区域，独立优化的重建也会收敛到不同的基元配置，导致变化检测等下游任务产生大量误报。需要一种对欠约束表示鲁棒、能直接捕获视觉行为而忽略朗伯纹理的特征表示。

Method: 从3DGS表示中推导出闭式解析光场导数（plenoptic derivatives），构建PlenoCI特征。该特征直接编码5D光场结构（即视角依赖的视觉行为），同时通过构造忽略朗伯纹理，对欠约束重建天然鲁棒。将其集成到实例感知的3DGS管线中用于变化检测，并支持几何/外观变化分类。

Result: 在CL-Splats基准上实现25.7% mIoU增益超过最强竞争对手（达到SOTA），在PASLCD基准上保持竞争力；在独立重建的未变化场景中报告比其他方法少两个数量级的误报；几何/外观变化分类平衡准确率达0.735，与最佳基线相当。

Conclusion: Plenoptic derivatives和PlenoCI为视觉复杂环境中视角依赖感知的理解开辟了新方向，证明了光场导数特征在欠约束辐射场表示上做可靠变化检测和分类的有效性。

Abstract: Radiance field representations such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) natively encode complex visual phenomena such as occlusions and view dependence, but they are inherently underconstrained. Independently optimized reconstructions converge to different primitive configurations, even in unchanged regions. We introduce Plenoptic CharacterIstics (PlenoCI), a novel feature built from the plenoptic field these representations approximate. PlenoCI directly captures rich visual behaviors while ignoring Lambertian textures. By deriving closed-form analytic plenoptic derivatives from a 3DGS representation, we efficiently detect these 5D structures. Our approach is robust to underconstrained representations by construction, reporting two orders of magnitude fewer false positives between independent reconstructions of unchanged scenes than concurrent work. We demonstrate PlenoCI's utility on change classification. First, we detect changes with an instance-aware 3DGS pipeline, achieving state-of-the-art results on CL-Splats with a 25.7% mIoU gain over the strongest competitor, while remaining competitive on the more challenging PASLCD benchmark. Leveraging PlenoCI, we classify changes as geometric or appearance-based with a balanced accuracy of 0.735, comparable to the best performing baseline. We believe plenoptic derivatives and PlenoCI open new directions for view dependence aware understanding in visually complex environments. Code and data are available at https://js0n-lai.github.io/plenoci.

</details>


### [19] [HelloWorld: Towards Practical Applications of Generative Driving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28931)
*Fan Lu,Hanshi Wang,Zijing Wang,Quan Feng,Zhi Wang,Shijie Chen,Xianming Zeng,Yujian Zhang,Jiazhe Wang,Xin Zha,Kai Wang,Zhijie Zhao,Lin Zhu,Tianyi Yang,Yucheng Xu,Tao Ji,Haodong Zhang,Zhipeng Zhang,Peixi Peng,Guang Chen,Xingliang Liu,Lei Yang,Jianyun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HelloWorld是一个2B参数规模的驾驶世界模型系统，通过渐进式特化异构视频中的视觉与运动先验，利用自车姿态、HD地图和3D框实现可控驾驶生成，支持七相机RGB同步生成与条件LiDAR合成，并蒸馏至少步推理实现高效部署，为可扩展驾驶数据生成和交互式仿真提供统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶世界模型有望实现超越录制的驾驶日志的可扩展反事实数据生成与交互式仿真，但实际落地需要系统能够跨不同场景泛化、对规定控制指令做出忠实响应、生成连贯的多传感器观测，并在重复推理下高效运行。现有方法尚未能同时满足这些要求。

Method: HelloWorld采用渐进式特化策略，从异构视频数据中获取广泛的视觉与运动先验，再利用自车姿态（ego pose）、HD地图和3D包围框将模型适配到可控驾驶生成任务。系统引入块因果（block-causal）生成接口，并通过适配自生成上下文（self-generated context）来对齐顺序仿真需求。此外，系统支持七相机同步RGB生成和条件LiDAR合成，并通过蒸馏实现少步推理以提升部署效率。

Result: HelloWorld在视觉质量、控制保真度、跨视角一致性、重复生成下的鲁棒性、推理效率以及LiDAR合成等多个维度上均进行了实验验证，取得了系统性成果，展示了其在驾驶数据生成与交互式仿真方面的综合能力。

Conclusion: HelloWorld为可扩展的驾驶数据生成与交互式仿真提供了一个统一框架，兼顾了生成质量、控制响应、多传感器一致性以及部署效率，验证了驾驶世界模型在实际应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Driving world models provide a promising route toward scalable counterfactual data generation and interactive simulation beyond recorded driving logs. Realizing this potential requires a system that can generalize across diverse scenes, respond faithfully to prescribed controls, generate coherent multi-sensor observations, and operate efficiently under repeated inference. We present \textbf{HelloWorld}, a 2B driving world model system designed around these requirements. HelloWorld progressively specializes broad visual and motion priors from heterogeneous video data into controllable driving generation using ego pose, HD maps, and 3D boxes. A block-causal generation interface, together with adaptation to self-generated context, aligns the model with sequential simulation. The system further supports synchronized seven-camera RGB generation and conditional LiDAR synthesis, and is distilled toward few-step inference for efficient deployment. Experiments evaluate visual quality, control fidelity, cross-view consistency, robustness under repeated generation, inference efficiency, and LiDAR synthesis. Together, HelloWorld provides a unified framework for scalable driving data generation and interactive simulation.

</details>


### [20] [Exploiting Target Knowledge from MLLMs for Robust Few-Shot Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28949)
*Yijun Hu,Heng Fan,Libo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MK-FSS框架，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）从查询图像中挖掘两类互补的目标知识（空间知识和语义知识）来增强少样本分割（FSS）性能。其中空间知识通过记忆编码与支持引导的记忆特征经双记忆辩论融合（DMDF）模块整合，语义知识则经渐进式跨模态提示生成器（PCPG）与多尺度查询特征融合，最终生成目标感知的多模态提示用于分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的少样本分割方法依赖于支持图像与查询图像之间的外观匹配，在面对显著的外观差异和遮挡时表现不佳，原因是缺乏对目标类别的充分认知。现有方法未能充分利用大语言模型的强大推理能力来挖掘目标知识。

Method: MK-FSS基于SAM 2构建，利用MLLM从查询图像挖掘两种互补知识：空间知识（提供目标潜在位置的空间先验）和语义知识（用文本描述目标）。空间知识被编码为记忆表征，经由双记忆辩论融合（DMDF）模块与支持引导的记忆特征整合；语义知识被编码为文本特征，经由渐进式跨模态提示生成器（PCPG）与多尺度查询特征融合，生成目标感知的多模态提示。

Result: 大量实验表明MK-FSS取得了有前景的结果，大幅超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 双记忆特征与多模态提示共同提供了对目标的全面表征，实现了更鲁棒的少样本分割，代码将开源发布。

Abstract: Few-shot segmentation (FSS) aims to segment unseen object categories with a few (e.g., one or five) labeled examples, enabling efficient adaptation to novel classes. Conventional models typically rely on appearance-based visual matching between support and query images for segmentation. While straightforward, these methods often struggle to handle significant appearance discrepancies and occlusions in the query image due to insufficient target knowledge. To mitigate this, we introduce a novel framework that mines target knowledge using the strong reasoning capacity of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and employs it to enhance FSS. Specifically, building on SAM 2, our method, named MK-FSS, exploits two forms of complementary knowledge derived from a query image by an MLLM for FSS, including spatial knowledge, which provides a spatial prior indicating the potential target location, and semantic knowledge, which describes the target using text. The spatial knowledge is first encoded into a memory representation, and then resulting memory is integrated with the support-guided memory feature from query image through a carefully designed dual-memory debate-fusion (DMDF) module, yielding a more robust target memory feature. In parallel, the semantic knowledge is encoded into the textual feature, which is fused with multi-scale query features via a progressive cross-modal prompt generator (PCPG), producing a target-aware multimodal prompt for segmentation. Working together, the dual-memory feature and the multimodal prompt provide a comprehensive representation of the target, enabling more robust segmentation. In our extensive experiments, MK-FSS shows promising results and largely surpasses existing methods. Code will be released.

</details>


### [21] [MoVISA: Multi-Token Reasoning for Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28956)
*Ruining Zhao,Ho Kei Cheng,Alexander G Schwing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoVISA提出了一种基于多分词(Multi-Token)推理的视频目标分割方法，使用多个分割token（如SEG0、SEG1）来表示不同帧中的同一对象，实现了更细粒度的语言提示与时空掩码预测对齐，在MeViS上J&F提升13.2%，在ReVOS上提升8.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的视频目标分割方法通常使用单一文本token（如SEG）来预测分割掩码，但作者观察到这种单token策略缺乏足够粒度，难以在视频中跨时间精确定位多个对象。

Method: MoVISA采用多个分割token（如SEG0和SEG1）来表示不同帧中的对象，从而实现语言提示与时空掩码预测之间更细粒度的对齐，提高分割性能和可解释性。

Result: 在MeViS、DAVIS17、ReVOS和Ref-Youtube-VOS四个基准上取得显著提升，其中MeViS上J&F提升13.2%，ReVOS上提升8.4%。

Conclusion: 多token推理策略能够有效解决单token分割在视频多对象精确定位上的粒度不足问题，且代码和模型将开源发布。

Abstract: Recent advances in video object segmentation with Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) reasoning have demonstrated the effectiveness of using a single textual token, such as SEG, to predict segmentation masks across images and videos. However, we observe that this single-token strategy lacks the granularity required to precisely localize multiple objects across time in video segmentation tasks. To address this limitation, we develop Multi-Token Reasoning for Video Object Segmentation, or MoVISA. MoVISA uses multiple segmentation tokens, such as SEG0 and SEG1, to represent an object across different frames. This design enables more fine-grained alignment between language prompts and spatio-temporal mask predictions, improving both performance and interpretability. On the challenging MeViS, DAVIS17, ReVOS, and Ref-Youtube-VOS benchmarks, our model achieves a 13.2 percent J and F improvement on MeViS and an 8.4 percent J and F improvement on ReVOS. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [22] [Passive LWIR Hyperspectral Ranging via Transmittance Extraction and Distance Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28967)
*Zhihe Chen,Chen Fan,Shuo Liu,Xiaolin Huang,Yunze He,Xiaofeng He,Lilian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TEDA（透射率提取与距离对齐）方法，用于被动长波红外高光谱测距中快速且准确的远距离估计。TEDA将距离估计与温度-发射率反演解耦：第一阶段通过闭合形式的平滑分支和观测导出的门控融合提取大气透射率，第二阶段将恢复的透射率与逐候选距离的传感器域透射率模型匹配来完成测距。相比参考距离联合反演，TEDA在蒙特卡洛仿真中减小了测距偏差，在实测场景中均值测距更接近LiDAR中位数，且运算速度提高约20倍（8.19s vs 159.47s）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动长波红外高光谱测距通过在低照度和夜间场景中利用大气吸收特征估计距离，但联合估计温度、发射率和距离的计算成本高昂；且参考距离联合反演采用距离不变的有效衰减系数，会引入测距偏差。因此需要一种计算高效且偏差更小的测距方法。

Method: TEDA采用两阶段解耦策略。第一阶段：设计对已知吸收方向不变的数据保真项，获得两条针对缓慢变化热连续背景的闭合形式平滑分支（baseline estimator），通过观测导出的门控将分支融合，再在对数域中减去融合后的基线以恢复大气透射率。第二阶段：将恢复的透射率与针对每个候选距离重新计算的传感器域透射率模型进行匹配，从而估计距离。这一设计避免了联合反演中距离不变衰减系数的近似。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真表明TEDA能有效减小由距离不变衰减系数近似引起的测距偏差。在实测场景中，TEDA在两个评估区域的均值测距估计均比参考距离联合反演更接近LiDAR中位数（即更准确）。在处理完整256×256感兴趣区域时，TEDA耗时8.19秒，而参考距离联合反演需159.47秒，实现了约20倍的加速。

Conclusion: TEDA通过将距离估计从温度-发射率反演中解耦，在保持测距准确性的同时显著提升了计算效率（约20倍加速），并有效消除了距离不变衰减系数近似带来的系统测距偏差，为低照度/夜间场景的高光谱测距提供了一种更实用高效的方案。

Abstract: Passive long-wave infrared (LWIR) hyperspectral ranging enables distance estimation in low-light and nighttime scenes by exploiting atmospheric absorption features in thermal radiance received through the atmosphere.Joint estimation of temperature, emissivity, and distance is computationally expensive. Reference-range joint inversion also uses a distance-invariant effective attenuation coefficient, which can bias range estimates.We introduce transmittance extraction and distance alignment (TEDA), which decouples range estimation from temperature--emissivity inversion. In the first stage, a baseline estimator with a data-fidelity term invariant to the known absorption direction yields two closed-form smoothing branches for the slowly varying thermal continuum. An observation-derived gate combines the branches, and subtracting the blended baseline in the log domain recovers atmospheric transmittance. The second stage estimates range by matching the recovered transmittance to sensor-domain transmittance models recomputed for each candidate distance. Monte Carlo simulations show that TEDA effectively reduces the ranging bias caused by the distance-invariant attenuation coefficient approximation. In a measured scene, TEDA's mean range estimates are closer to the LiDAR medians than those of reference-range joint inversion in both evaluated patches. TEDA processes a complete $256\times256$ region of interest in 8.19~s versus 159.47~s for reference-range joint inversion, an approximately 20-fold speedup.

</details>


### [23] [Beneath the Scores: Rethinking Hallucination Evaluation for Video Understanding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28991)
*Shuzhi Gong,Fengze Sun,Yuansan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对多阶段LLM视频智能体的时序定位、视觉观察与推理三个阶段，提出一种因果阶段干预协议，在60,008次运行实验中揭示：时序定位（grounding）是下游幻觉错误的主导来源（因果影响约为视觉观察被破坏时的4倍），且定位成败主要取决于区域定位而非精确时间重叠；错误证据比缺失证据危害更大；现有基准分数无法可靠预测因果级联敏感性，并可能在分布偏移下失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段LLM智能体将视频理解拆分为时序定位、视觉观察和推理，但各阶段通常在不同基准和分布上评估，难以定位幻觉源头；现有阶段级分数信号不一致——更强的阶段性能并不可靠地意味着更低的下游幻觉，甚至针对同一能力的基准也会互相矛盾，因此需要因果干预来定位错误来源并审计现有基准的有效性。

Method: 提出因果阶段干预协议：在保持下游任务固定的前提下，分别覆盖（overwrite）单个阶段以度量其对下游的因果影响。在三种视频智能体架构上执行60,008次运行，并通过审计现有基准与干预结果的一致性来检验其诊断价值，同时分析定位区域vs时间重叠、错误证据vs缺失证据等细粒度因素。

Result: 发现时序定位是下游错误的主导来源，其因果影响约为视觉观察被破坏的4倍；定位成功主要取决于定位到正确区域而非精确mIoU时间重叠，解释了标准mIoU指标对下游可靠性的预测能力较差；错误证据比缺失证据危害显著更大；现有基准分数无法可靠预测因果级联敏感性，并在分布偏移下失效。

Conclusion: 现有基于阶段分数的评估不能可靠反映下游可靠性，倡导采用基于因果干预、阶段感知（stage-aware）的评估协议来保障值得信赖的视频智能体。

Abstract: Video understanding is increasingly performed by multi-stage LLM agents that separate temporal grounding, visual observation, and reasoning. Yet these stages are typically evaluated on different benchmarks and distributions, making it difficult to determine where hallucinations originate. We first organize existing benchmarks around these stages and show that their scores provide inconsistent diagnostic signals: stronger stage-level performance does not reliably imply lower downstream hallucination, and even benchmarks targeting the same capability can disagree.
  We therefore introduce a causal stage-intervention protocol that overwrites individual stages while holding the downstream task fixed. Across 60,008 runs on three video-agent architectures, we find that grounding is the dominant source of downstream error, with roughly four times the causal impact of corrupting visual observations. Successful grounding depends primarily on locating the correct region rather than precise temporal overlap, explaining why standard mIoU metrics poorly predict downstream reliability. We further find that incorrect evidence is substantially more harmful than missing evidence. Finally, auditing existing benchmarks against these interventions reveals that their scores do not reliably predict causal cascade sensitivity and can fail under distribution shift. These results motivate intervention-based, stage-aware evaluation for trustworthy video agents.

</details>


### [24] [Only What Was Seen: Observation-Gram Compaction of View-Dependent Appearance in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28997)
*Krzysztof Pietroszek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于观测Gram矩阵的失真度量，用于3D高斯溅射模型压缩。该度量利用每个高斯仅从训练相机窄锥方向被观测的特性，构建从球谐系数变化到平方图像误差的一阶精确映射，仅需模型和相机位姿即可计算。在此度量下，阶数降低成为闭式投影、阶数分配成为率失真问题、向量量化成为矩阵加权Lloyd算法。集成到Compressed3D中无需其他改动即可提升PSNR +0.49 dB，微调前在相同码率下仍获+0.32 dB增益；基于该度量的免训练堆栈在Mip-NeRF 360数据集上比GSICO小15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯溅射模型的绝大多数内存用于存储球谐颜色系数，但每个高斯实际上仅从训练相机的窄锥方向被观测，现有压缩方法未充分利用这一几何先验。作者旨在设计一种可被其他压缩器采用的失真度量，精确刻画系数变化对图像误差的影响。

Method: 提出每高斯观测Gram矩阵，由观测方向和混合权重累积而成，它是从系数变化到平方图像误差的一阶精确映射。基于该度量：(1) 阶数降低转化为闭式投影（泛化截断）；(2) 阶数分配转化为Lagrangian率失真问题；(3) 向量量化成为矩阵加权Lloyd算法，其中Compressed3D的量化器为其标量特例。

Result: 将该度量替换进Compressed3D且不改其他组件，微调前PSNR提升+0.49 dB，SSIM和LPIPS同步改善；在匹配码率下无需任何训练图像仍获得+0.32 dB增益。基于纯度量的免训练堆栈在Mip-NeRF 360上同等质量下比GSICO小15%。

Conclusion: 观测Gram矩阵失真度量提供了一种无需训练即可精确指导3D高斯溅射压缩的理论框架，可作为通用基础模块嵌入现有压缩方法，显著提升率失真性能，展示了利用观测几何先验进行模型压缩的有效性。

Abstract: Most of the memory of a 3D Gaussian Splatting model holds spherical-harmonic colour coefficients, yet each Gaussian is seen only from the narrow cone of directions of the training cameras. We turn this into a distortion metric that other compressors can adopt: a per-Gaussian observation Gram matrix, accumulated from viewing directions and blending weights, is the exact first-order map from coefficient changes to squared image error and needs only the model and the camera poses. Under it, degree reduction becomes a closed-form projection that generalises truncation, degree allocation a Lagrangian rate-distortion problem, and vector quantisation the matrix-weighted Lloyd algorithm, of which Compressed3D's quantiser is the scalar case. Swapped into Compressed3D with everything else unchanged, the metric raises PSNR by +0.49 dB before fine-tuning, with SSIM and LPIPS following, and at matched rate still gains +0.32 dB without a single training image. A training-free stack built on the metric alone is 15% smaller than the image-free GSICO at equal quality on Mip-NeRF 360.

</details>


### [25] [FluidRain: Incompressible Rain Flow as an Attention Bias for Loop-in-Loop Video Deraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29006)
*Pu Wang,Yongcong Wang,Wenhao Li,Xiang Chen,Guangwei Gao,Jinshan Pan,Siyuan Yao,Shujun Fu,Zhuoran Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FluidRain是一个轻量级视频去雨模型，利用无散度（divergence-free）雨流场来引导跨尺度和相邻帧的Loop-in-Loop注意力机制，无需显式对齐即可实现可靠的时间聚合。该模型仅用0.80M参数，在四个基准上达到与大型模型相当的效果，并引入RainSyn-Gust数据集和基于物理的无参考评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频去雨方法分为显式对齐（依赖精确运动估计，在密集雨天下不可靠）和隐式时空聚合（缺乏对雨的方向性和时间相干性结构的显式引导）。这导致可靠的时间聚合与雨运动显式建模之间存在空白。

Method: 受流体力学启发，将雨运动建模为图像空间的散度自由流场。首先为每帧估计雨流场并投影到无散度子空间，该流场引导窗口注意力沿雨条纹方向移动，使相邻帧无需显式对齐即可聚合。由于雨流结构在尺度和相邻帧间保持，Loop-in-Loop机制在两个维度上复用同一注意力算子，形成仅0.80M参数的三帧模型。同时提出RainSyn-Gust基准以注入雨条纹方向的变化，并开发基于物理的无参考评价指标。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，FluidRain以0.80M参数的极小规模保持与大幅更大的恢复模型相近的竞争力。此外，RainSyn-Gust用于评估雨运动变化时模型的可靠性，基于物理的无参考指标可评估真实雨去除效果而不需干净参考图。

Conclusion: FluidRain通过无散度雨流引导的Loop-in-Loop注意力，在无需显式对齐的情况下实现可靠的跨帧时间聚合，兼顾了轻量级参数与强大去雨性能，并提供了应对雨运动变化的数据集与无参考评价工具。

Abstract: Existing video deraining methods typically exploit neighboring frames through either explicit alignment or implicit spatiotemporal aggregation. Explicit alignment relies on accurate motion estimation, which can become unreliable under dense rain, while implicit aggregation avoids alignment but lacks explicit guidance on the directional and temporally coherent structure of rain. This leaves a gap between reliable temporal aggregation and explicit modeling of rain motion. To address these limitations, we propose FluidRain, a lightweight video derainer that uses divergence-free rain flow to guide Loop-in-Loop attention across scales and neighboring frames. Motivated by fluid mechanics, we model rain motion as a divergence-free image-space flow and use it to organize multi-scale and temporal aggregation. Specifically, FluidRain first estimates a rain-flow field for each frame and projects it onto the divergence-free subspace. The resulting flow steers window attention along rain streaks, enabling neighboring frames to be aggregated without explicit alignment. Since rain-flow structure is preserved across scales and nearby frames, Loop-in-Loop reuses the same attention operator across both dimensions, resulting in a three-frame model with only 0.80M parameters. Experiments on four benchmarks show that FluidRain remains competitive with substantially larger restoration models. We further examine how temporal evidence scales with different input views. To evaluate whether the model remains reliable when rain motion changes across frames, we introduce RainSyn-Gust, which injects controlled changes in rain-streak direction into existing benchmarks. We also develop a physics-based no-reference metric that evaluates real-rain removal without requiring clean targets.

</details>


### [26] [RGBD20K: A Large-Scale Benchmark for RGB-D Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29028)
*Shaohua Dong,Zexuan Meng,Haiyan Sun,Bing Fan,Cuicui Zhang,Dylan Joseph,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng,Heng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RGBD20K数据集，包含20,000对RGB-D图像和160个细粒度类别，远超现有基准（NYUv2的40类和SUN RGB-D的37类）。该数据集通过严格重新评估和修正现有标签以消除标注噪声，并提供高保真标注。此外，论文提出了一种新颖的分数纯化融合（SPF）方法，在所有评估基准上达到最先进性能，验证了利用高质量多模态信息进行RGB-D语义分割的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-D语义分割基准数据集的语义类别数量有限（如NYUv2仅40类、SUN RGB-D仅37类），制约了分割模型的泛化能力；同时现有标注存在长期噪声问题。因此需要构建一个类别丰富、规模更大、标注质量更高的数据集来促进更鲁棒和通用的RGB-D语义分割模型的发展。

Method: (1) 构建RGBD20K数据集：扩展语义空间至160个细粒度类别，规模达20,000对RGB-D图像，并对现有标签进行严格的重新评估与修正以获得高保真标注。(2) 提出分数纯化融合（SPF）方法：通过分数纯化机制有效融合RGB和深度模态的多模态信息，提升分割性能。

Result: RGBD20K在类别多样性（160类）和规模（20,000对）上大幅超越现有基准；SPF方法在所有评估基准上均取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的分割性能。

Conclusion: RGBD20K作为类别丰富、规模大且标注高质量的数据集，能够有效促进更通用和鲁棒的RGB-D语义分割模型学习；所提出的SPF融合方法证明了高质量多模态信息利用的有效性，为RGB-D语义分割领域提供了重要的数据和算法贡献。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose RGBD20K, a novel dataset for facilitating the development of more robust and general RGB-D semantic segmentation by encompassing abundant categories and high-quality annotations. RGBD20K possesses several attractive properties: (1) Expanded Semantic Space. In particular, it covers 160 fine-grained categories, largely surpassing the category diversity of existing popular RGB-D benchmarks (e.g., NYUv2 with 40 classes and SUN RGB-D with 37 classes). With such enriched semantic coverage, we expect to promote the learning of more generalizable segmentation models. (2) Larger Scale. Compared with current benchmarks, RGBD20K offers 20,000 RGB-D image pairs, providing a substantially larger training resource that benefits the development of more powerful deep models. (3) High-Fidelity Annotation. We perform rigorous re-evaluation and correction of existing labels to resolve long-standing annotation noise, resulting in a clean and reliable ground-truth foundation. Furthermore, we propose a novel score-purified fusion (SPF) method, which achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated benchmarks, demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach in leveraging high-quality multimodal information for RGB-D semantic segmentation. The dataset is here: https://github.com/ShaohuaDong2021/RGBD20K/.

</details>


### [27] [Exploiting answer-invariant redundancies in satellite imagery for efficient VLM inference on edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29029)
*Ishani Janveja,Davis Zhang,Seoyul Oh,Deepak Vasisht*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Rift是一个两阶段系统，通过查询条件化的瓦片剪枝和弹性预填充来减少高分辨率卫星图像的视觉token冗余（AITR），在Jetson AGX Orin上运行LLaVA-1.5 7B，相比穷举瓦片推理降低78%能耗和69%延迟，同时将准确率从45%提升到73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载视觉语言模型（VLM）可以直接回答卫星图像查询，但对高分辨率图像进行穷举式瓦片推理速度慢且能耗高，不适合星载资源受限环境。

Method: 提出Rift，一个两阶段系统：(1) 查询条件化的瓦片剪枝，移除与最终答案无关的图像瓦片；(2) 弹性预填充（elastic prefill），进一步减少vision token预算。核心基于AITR（答案不变token冗余）概念——移除后不改变最终答案的图像瓦片和视觉token。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5 7B + Jetson AGX Orin平台上，相比穷举瓦片推理，Rift减少78%能耗、69%延迟，并将准确率从45%提升至73%。

Conclusion: 通过识别并利用AITR，Rift在显著降低星载VLM推理的资源和时间开销的同时，还提升了准确率，证明了查询条件化token削减在高分辨率遥感图像理解中的有效性。

Abstract: Onboard vision-language models could enable satellites to answer queries directly, but exhaustive tiled inference over high-resolution imagery is slow and energy-intensive. We identify answer-invariant token redundancy (AITR): image tiles and vision tokens that can be removed without changing the final answer. We present Rift, a two-stage system that performs query-conditioned tile pruning followed by elastic prefill to reduce token budget. We evaluate it on LLaVA-1.5 7B running on Jetson AGX Orin. Compared with exhaustive tiled inference, Rift reduces energy by 78% and latency by 69%, while increasing accuracy from 45% to 73%.

</details>


### [28] [Where Hallucinations Live: A Cross-Architecture Circuit in VQ-Tokenized Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29048)
*Shamanthak Hegde,Xiangrui Liu,Maitreya Patel,Yezhou Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过激活修补技术跨25个模型（涵盖8个LLM家族）识别出统一VQ标记化VLM中共享的早期层（L0）注意力路由电路，提出三门诊断方法区分携带该电路的模型（10个阳性）与不携带的模型（15个阴性）。通过单变量架构替换证明向量量化（VQ）是病理信号的来源，并发现只有L0消融能在开放式生成中减少物体幻觉（CHAIRi相对降低31%），而VCD和DoLA基线无法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一视觉-语言模型（VLM）在基于事实的是/否基准上经常产生物体幻觉，现有解码时修复方法将其视为通用校准问题，缺乏架构层面的解释。作者希望从架构和预训练角度阐明幻觉的根本原因，并开发更有针对性的干预方法。

Method: 使用激活修补（activation patching）技术，跨25个模型（8个LLM家族）进行分析，识别早期层L0注意力路由电路。提出三门诊断（three-gate diagnostic）来区分携带与不携带该电路的模型。通过单变量架构替换（LLaVA-1.6 CLIP+MLP→VQ+Linear）验证因果性，并与匹配计算量的MLP对照实验隔离向量量化为病理源。对比L0消融与调优后的VCD和DoLA基线。

Result: 诊断方法识别出10个阳性模型（5个自然统一VQ-VLM跨3个LLM家族，5个诱导变体），排除其余15个。单变量架构替换确认VQ是电路来源。在开放式生成中，只有L0消融显著降低物体幻觉（CHAIRi相对降低31%），而调优的DoLA在二分类校准上更好但对CHAIR无效，调优VCD甚至使情况恶化。

Conclusion: 统一VQ标记化VLM中的物体幻觉是架构和预训练的固有属性，而非简单的校准问题。L0消融提供了一种机制感知的定向干预，这是机制无关的解码方法（如VCD和DoLA）无法复制的。

Abstract: Unified vision-language models (VLMs) that tokenize images through a vector-quantized (VQ) codebook routinely hallucinate objects on grounded yes/no benchmarks, yet existing decoding-time fixes treat this as generic miscalibration without an architectural account. Using activation patching across twenty-five models spanning eight LLM families, we identify an early-layer ($L_0$) attention routing circuit shared across VQ-tokenized VLMs and propose a three-gate diagnostic that distinguishes the models carrying it from those that do not. The diagnostic isolates ten positive models (five natural unified-VQ VLMs across three LLM families and five induced variants) and rejects the remaining fifteen. A single-variable architectural swap (LLaVA-1.6 CLIP+MLP $\rightarrow$ VQ+Linear) installs the circuit, while a matched-compute MLP control on identical data does not, isolating vector quantization as the source of the pathological signal; the routing pathway that carries it is one that the backbone already provides. Against tuned VCD and DoLA baselines, tuned DoLA wins on binary calibration, but \textbf{only $L_0$ ablation reduces object hallucination in open-ended generation} (CHAIR$_i$ reduces by $31\,\%$ relatively, whereas tuned DoLA and VCD leave it unchanged or worsen it). These results recast object hallucination in unified VQ VLMs as a property of architecture and pretraining, and yield a targeted intervention that mechanism-agnostic decoding cannot replicate.

</details>


### [29] [EIB-Net: Entropy-Guided Information Bottleneck for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29064)
*Zhida Zhang,Xinlei Ma,Jie Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EIB-Net（熵引导信息瓶颈网络），利用生成模型偏向全局语义而牺牲局部纹理保真度的特性，通过图像熵（IE）指标自动选取信息量最高（熵值最低）的补丁，并结合变分信息瓶颈（VIB）学习紧凑可泛化特征，实现高效、稳健的AI生成图像检测，仅用2%训练数据即达85.7%的准确率，并在跨生成器泛化上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测方法主要针对基于操纵的伪造图像（依赖局部伪影），而基于生成（如扩散模型）的图像缺乏此类痕迹，构成根本性挑战。作者观察到生成模型在生成过程中优先保留全局语义而牺牲局部纹理保真度，因此低纹理区域成为识别合成图像的关键线索，据此构建检测框架。

Method: 提出EIB-Net：1) 引入图像熵（IE）指标自动选择信息量最高（最低熵）的图像补丁作为关键判别区域；2) 采用变分信息瓶颈（VIB）处理所选补丁，学习紧凑且可泛化的特征表示；3) 熵引导补丁选择策略（EGPL）可独立嵌入不同骨干网络（CNN与Transformer）以提升性能。

Result: 在DIFF、DiffusionForensics和GenImage基准上达到最先进性能：仅使用2%训练数据即取得85.7%准确率，比全图基线高出15%以上；在GenImage上保持跨生成器泛化能力（83.5%平均准确率）。熵引导补丁选择（EGPL）能一致提升多种骨干网络性能，验证其数据高效检测的实用价值。

Conclusion: EIB-Net通过挖掘生成模型在低纹理区域暴露的痕迹，结合熵引导补丁选择与信息瓶颈机制，实现了数据高效且强泛化的AI生成图像检测。该方法在极少训练数据下仍显著超越全图基线，且EGPL策略具有通用性，可为后续生成图像检测提供一个实用且轻量的解决方案。

Abstract: The proliferation of photorealistic AI-generated images demands robust detection methods that generalize across diverse generative models. While existing approaches target manipulation-based forgeries with local artifacts, generation-based images (e.g., from diffusion models) lack such traces, posing a fundamental challenge. We observe that generative models prioritize global semantics at the expense of local texture fidelity, making low-texture regions key indicators of synthetic origin. To exploit this, we propose EIB-Net, an Entropy-guided Information Bottleneck Network. EIB-Net introduces a novel Image Entropy (IE) metric to automatically select the most informative (lowest-entropy) patch, then processes it with a Variational Information Bottleneck (VIB) to learn compact, generalizable features. Extensive experiments on DIFF, DiffusionForensics, and GenImage benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: EIB-Net achieves 85.7\% accuracy using only 2\% of training data, outperforming full-image baselines by over 15\%, and maintains robust cross-generator generalization (83.5\% average accuracy on GenImage). Furthermore, our entropy-guided patch selection (EGPL) consistently enhances diverse backbones (CNNs and Transformers), proving its practical value for data-efficient detection.

</details>


### [30] [Seeing Is Not Measuring: Tool-Augmented Metric Spatial Reasoning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29073)
*Kai Glantz,Clemens Grange*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化、与预测器无关、工具增强的框架，为小型VLM（Qwen3.5-4B）配备3D目标检测、度量深度估计和确定性求解器，将度量3D结构计算从模型权重中移出，提升ReVSI-Bench三/四项任务的度量推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型擅长场景描述，但在绝对距离、物理尺寸、自我中心方向等度量3D结构推理上表现不佳，因此需要外部几何工具弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 采用模块化、预测器无关的工具增强框架：每个目标在自身最佳视角的相机坐标系中检测，利用该视角位姿将检测结果统一到共享世界坐标系，再由确定性求解器计算距离、尺寸和方位；支持任意检测器替换。

Result: 在ReVSI-Bench四项任务中三项大幅提升：绝对距离MRA从0.46升至0.74，相对距离从39.1%升至67.4%，相对方向从低于随机水平的25.9%升至73.4%。使用真实检测器与ground-truth框对比，编排代价仅0.03 MRA；物体尺寸受限于检测器（ground-truth框0.97，最佳真实检测器0.61 vs基线0.58）。模型可自行正确编排工具序列，三/四任务匹配脚本流程。

Conclusion: 显式求解器与外部工具能显著提升VLM的度量3D推理能力，主要误差来源于单目检测器的尺寸估计；框架的模块化设计便于分离感知误差与推理误差，且模型具备自主工具编排能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) describe scenes well but reason poorly about metric 3D structure such as absolute distances, physical sizes, or egocentric directions. We present a modular, predictor agnostic, tool-augmented framework that equips a small VLM (Qwen3.5-4B) with geometric tools: 3D object detection, metric depth estimation, and deterministic solvers for distance, size and bearing. Each object is detected in the camera frame of its own best view, and the tools use that frame's pose to lift every detection into one shared world frame. Moving metric computation out of the model's weights and into explicit solvers yields large gains on three of four ReVSI-Bench tasks: with a strong monocular detector (WildDet3D), absolute distance rises from 0.46 to 0.74 Mean Relative Accuracy (MRA), relative distance from 39.1% to 67.4%, and relative direction from a below-chance 25.9% to 73.4%. Because any detector can be swapped in behind the tool interface, comparing real detectors against ground-truth boxes separates perception error from reasoning error: orchestration costs only 0.03 MRA. Object size is bounded by the detector: the tools are near-exact on groundtruth boxes (0.97) yet the best real detector barely beats the no-tool baseline (0.61 vs. 0.58), because size reads straight off a box extent monocular detectors get wrong. Without a predefined recipe, the model already sequences the tools correctly on its own, matching a scripted pipeline on three of four tasks.

</details>


### [31] [WildHSR: Metric Feed-Forward 4D People-Scene Reconstruction from a 3D Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29106)
*Jerrin Bright,John Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WildHSR，通过轻量适配的方式，从一个up-to-scale的3D基础模型表示中同时恢复公制尺度（metric scale）和人物身份（person identity），实现单目视频中的公制人物-场景联合重建。该方法利用互联网视频中的人物伪标签预训练尺度读取头，从基础模型token直接预测公制尺度；同时利用基础模型中间层的query-key特征进行跨帧人物关联，通过Sinkhorn匹配实现时序上的人物身份持续跟踪。WildHSR在EMDB-2数据集上成为首个超越最优优化方法的feed-forward方法，在RICH数据集上也领先其他feed-forward人物-场景方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D基础模型可以一步前向恢复相机和几何，但最强的模型输出是up-to-scale的，缺少公制尺度和人物身份持续性两个关键输出。精确的公制标签稀缺而互联网视频丰富，作者希望探究能否通过轻量适配从一个up-to-scale基础表示同时获得这两个能力。

Method: 1) 尺度伪标签：利用精心挑选的网络视频中的人物，通过公制身体姿态和2D关键点闭合形式近似求解尺度伪标签，预训练Scale Readout头，再用标准真实视频训练集上的精确公制监督与轻量适配器联合微调。推理时直接从基础模型token预测公制尺度。2) 人物身份：探测发现基础模型中间层的query-key特征编码了跨帧人物对应关系，用小型投影层读取该对应关系，结合公制骨盆运动和提议置信度，驱动带dustbin的Sinkhorn关联实现逐帧身体的跨帧匹配。3) 窗口间通过解析关联和Sim(3)组合连接，实现全视频重建。

Result: 在EMDB-2上，WildHSR是已发表对比中首个在所有三个世界坐标系指标上超越最优优化方法的feed-forward方法（WA-MPJPE和RTE领先）；在RICH上，领先其他feed-forward人物-场景方法（WA-MPJPE和W-MPJPE）。完整pipeline在单GPU上运行速度达10.1 fps。

Conclusion: 研究证明了up-to-scale基础模型表示可以通过轻量适配同时支持公制尺度和人物身份两个任务：利用互联网视频的伪标签可以低成本地解决尺度问题，而基础模型本身已经隐式编码了人物对应信息。该方法实现了快速、前馈的公制人物-场景联合重建，超越了优化方法。

Abstract: 3D foundation models recover video cameras and geometry in one forward pass, but some of the strongest are up to scale. Joint people-scene reconstruction then requires two missing outputs: metric scale and persistent person identity. We ask whether one up-to-scale foundation representation can support both through lightweight adaptation. Exact metric labels are scarce, but unlabeled in-the-wild video is abundant. We use people in curated web video to initialise the solution: a posed metric body and 2D keypoints give an approximate, closed-form scale pseudo-label. These pseudo-labels pretrain a Scale Readout, which is then fine-tuned together with a lightweight adapter using exact metric supervision from standard real-video training splits. At inference the head predicts metric scale from foundation-model tokens, without the ruler or its teachers. For person identity, we probe the pretrained foundation model alone and find evidence that its intermediate query-key features encode person correspondence across frames. In most evaluated moving-person clips, a mid-layer token prefers that person over the vacated location and other people. A tiny projection reads this correspondence; together with metric pelvis motion and proposal confidence, it drives dustbin-aware Sinkhorn association of per-frame bodies. WildHSR combines both readouts to reconstruct metric cameras, scene and people from monocular video. Each window is predicted feed-forward; analytic association and Sim(3) composition connect windows. On EMDB-2, WildHSR is the first feed-forward method in the published comparison to beat the best optimization-based WA-MPJPE and RTE while leading feed-forward methods on all three world-frame metrics. On RICH, it leads feed-forward people-and-scene methods on WA-MPJPE and W-MPJPE. The complete pipeline runs at 10.1 fps on one GPU.

</details>


### [32] [Spectral Amplitude Purification in Distribution Matching for Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29116)
*Zhenyu Zhou,Can Wang,Chun Chen,Zeyu Zheng,Defang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAP-DMD（谱幅度纯化分布匹配蒸馏），针对DMD优化中低频分量占主导、阻碍高频细节恢复的问题，通过自适应调节DMD方向场的幅度谱、抑制低频主导尾部分量，从而加速训练收敛并提升2步/4步采样的生成质量。实验在PixArt-α、SD3和SD3.5上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD虽能以少步数实现高质量扩散采样，但其优化动态被粗粒度低频信号主导，导致细粒度细节恢复延迟。作者发现DMD方向误差的谱幅度在低频处高度集中，低频主导分量压制了中高频信号，这一谱失衡成为收敛速度与生成质量的瓶颈。

Method: 提出SAP-DMD，一种即插即用方法：自适应调节DMD方向场的幅度谱，通过抑制幅度谱中占主导的低频尾部，降低低频主导性，从而促进细结构和高频纹理的有效恢复。

Result: 在PixArt-α、SD3和SD3.5上实验表明，SAP-DMD在2步和4步采样下均能加速训练收敛并提升生成质量。

Conclusion: 谱幅度纯化是一种有效的干预手段，能缓解DMD优化中的低频主导问题，在少步采样场景下同时带来收敛速度与生成质量的提升，且作为即插即用模块具有通用性。

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) enables high-quality diffusion sampling in only a few steps, but its optimization dynamics remain dominated by coarse, low-frequency signals, delaying the recovery of fine-grained details. We identify a pronounced concentration of spectral amplitudes at low frequencies in the DMD directional error, where dominant low-frequency components overwhelm weaker mid- and high-frequency signals. To address this issue, we propose Spectral Amplitude Purification for Distribution Matching Distillation (SAP-DMD), a plug-and-play approach that adaptively modulates the amplitude spectrum of the DMD directional field. By suppressing the dominant tail of the amplitude spectrum, SAP-DMD reduces low-frequency dominance and promotes more effective recovery of fine structures and textures. Experiments on PixArt-$α$, SD3, and SD3.5 demonstrate that SAP-DMD accelerates training convergence and improves generation quality under both 2-step and 4-step sampling.

</details>


### [33] [UpDown-SC: Gravity-Canonicalized Dual-Envelope Scan Context for Indoor LiDAR Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29118)
*Jie Xu,Yongxin Yang,Ziyi Jin,Kangjin Yu,Hongjun Huang,Chao Han,Zhongpu Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UpDown-SC是一种无需训练的点云极坐标描述子，通过先规范化重力方向，再分别表示下层/中层结构的上包络和头顶结构的下包络，解决了室内LiDAR地点识别中因姿态或安装高度变化导致的检索困难问题，在室内及高度变化场景下显著优于Scan Context。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内LiDAR地点识别在扫描姿态或传感器安装高度变化时性能显著下降。现有Scan Context仅存储每个极坐标单元的最大高度，而室内宽阔的天花板会压制用于区分相邻房间和走廊的中低层几何信息，导致检索困难。

Method: 提出UpDown-SC，一种免训练极坐标描述子：(1) 首先对重力方向进行canonicalization规范化；(2) 用上下两个互补表面表征场景——下层/中层结构的上包络（upper envelope）与头顶结构的下包络（lower envelope）；(3) 物理分界面基于单元平衡的地图高度分布估计一次，供所有查询复用；(4) 采用掩码感知的非均匀双通道距离度量，保留具有判别力的低层证据同时限制跨会话变化的影响，不将未观测单元视为零高度；(5) 保留Scan Context的shortlisting和环形偏航对齐，检索假设可直接用于几何验证初始化。

Result: 在重复室内会话、安装高度变化、室外到室内混合轨迹及室外迁移序列上的实验表明：室内和高度变化会话中首选项检索更可靠；配对检验显示相较Scan Context在自建室内会话上有显著提升；在基于阈值的接受判定下取得最佳或次佳的F1max和AUPR；连续回放验证了检索假设支持度量级先验地图定位。

Conclusion: UpDown-SC通过分离上下互补表面并配合掩码感知的非均匀距离度量，在不增加训练成本的前提下有效缓解了室内场景中姿态/高度变化带来的地点识别退化问题，同时保持轻量级CPU前端特性，可直接支持前端回环检测与全局重定位。

Abstract: LiDAR place recognition is a key front end for loop closure and global relocalization, yet indoor retrieval remains difficult when attitude or sensor mounting height changes between mapping and query sessions. Scan Context stores the maximum height in each polar cell; indoors, broad ceilings can suppress the lower and mid-level geometry that distinguishes adjacent rooms and corridors. We present UpDown-SC, a training-free polar descriptor that first canonicalizes gravity and then represents two complementary surfaces: the upper envelope of lower/middle structures and the lower envelope of overhead structures. Their physical split is estimated once from a cell-balanced map height distribution and reused by every query. A mask-aware, non-uniform two-channel distance retains discriminative lower-level evidence while limiting sensitivity to its cross-session variation, without treating unobserved cells as zero-height measurements. Conventional Scan Context shortlisting and circular yaw alignment are retained, so retrieved hypotheses directly initialize geometric verification.
